1<\delta_{n}<n/C
, we have that

\max_{k=1,\ldots,K_{n}}\delta_{n}\max_{\lceil C\delta_{n}\rceil\leq j\leq n}\left\|\frac{1}{j}\sum_{i=1}^{j}Y_{nki}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\big((\log K_{n})^{2}\big).

Proof.

A union bound and Bernstein’s inequality, see Corollary 2.11 in
boucheron2013concentration
, yield that for any
A>0
,

\begin{split}&\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{k=1,\ldots,K_{n}}\delta_{n}\max_{\lceil C\delta_{n}\rceil\leq j\leq n}\left(\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{j}Y_{nki}}{j}\right)^{2}>A^{2}(\log K_{n})^{2}\right)\\
\leq&\sum_{k=1}^{K_{n}}\sum_{j=\lceil C\delta_{n}\rceil,\ldots,n}\left\{\mathbb{P}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{j}Y_{nki}>\frac{j}{\sqrt{\delta_{n}}}A\log K_{n}\right)+\mathbb{P}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{j}Y_{nki}<-\frac{j}{\sqrt{\delta_{n}}}A\log K_{n}\right)\right\}\\
\leq&2K_{n}\sum_{j=\lceil C\delta_{n}\rceil,\ldots,n}\exp\left(-\frac{j^{2}A^{2}(\log K_{n})^{2}/\delta_{n}}{2\big(j+cA\log(K_{n})j/\sqrt{\delta_{n}}\big)}\right).\end{split}

Moreover, there exist constants
B>0
and
D^{\prime}>0
not depending on
A
, such that for
D:=D^{\prime}A
,

\begin{split}&K_{n}\sum_{j=\lceil C\delta_{n}\rceil,\ldots,n}\exp\left(-\frac{j^{2}A^{2}(\log K_{n})^{2}/\delta_{n}}{2\big(j+cA\log(K_{n})j/\sqrt{\delta_{n}}\big)}\right)\leq K_{n}\sum_{j=\lceil C\delta_{n}\rceil,\ldots,n}\exp\left(-\frac{jA\log K_{n}}{B\sqrt{\delta_{n}}}\right)\\
\leq&K_{n}\sum_{j=\lceil C\delta_{n}\rceil,\ldots,n}\exp\left(-\sqrt{j}A\log(K_{n})/B\right)=\sum_{j=\lceil C\delta_{n}\rceil,\ldots,n}K_{n}^{-\sqrt{j}A/B+1}\\
\leq&\int_{1}^{\infty}\left(K_{n}^{-D}\right)^{\sqrt{x}}dx=\left[\frac{2\left(K_{n}^{-D}\right)^{\sqrt{x}}\left(-\sqrt{x}D\log K_{n}-1\right)}{D^{2}(\log K_{n})^{2}}\right]_{1}^{\infty}\\
=&\frac{2K_{n}^{-D}(D\log K_{n}+1)}{D^{2}(\log K_{n})^{2}}.\end{split}

The r.h.s. can be made arbitrarily small by choosing
A
and hence
D
large enough.
∎

Lemma 8
.

Let
(Y_{nj})_{n=1,2,\ldots,j=1,\ldots,n}
be independent (potentially multivariate) mean-zero random variables with
\mathbb{E}[\|Y_{nj}\|_{2}^{2}]\leq 1
and
\mathbb{E}\|Y_{nj}\|_{2}^{q}\leq\frac{q!}{2}c^{q-2}
for all
q\geq 3
and a constant
c>0
,
n\in\mathbb{N}
,
j=1,\ldots,n
. For any
L=1,\ldots,L_{\max}
, where
L_{\max}\geq 2
, let
0\leq\tau_{L,1,s}<\tau_{L,1,e}\leq\tau_{L,2,s}<\tau_{L,2,e}\leq\cdots\tau_{L,L_{\max},s}<\tau_{L,L_{\max},e}\leq n
be sequences of (random) time points that are independent of
(Y_{nj})_{n=1,2,\ldots,j=1,\ldots,n}
. Then,

\max_{L=1,\ldots,L_{\max}}\sum_{l=1}^{L_{\max}}\left(\tau_{L,l,e}-\tau_{L,l,s}\right)^{-1}\left\|\sum_{j=\tau_{L,l,s}+1}^{\tau_{L,l,e}}Y_{nj}\right\|_{2}^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\big((L_{\max}\log L_{\max})^{1/2}\big)

(72)

and

\begin{split}&\max_{L=1,\ldots,L_{\max}}\left|\sum_{l=1}^{L_{\max}}\left\{\left(\tau_{L,l,e}-\tau_{L,l,s}\right)^{-1}\sum_{j=\tau_{L,l,s}+1}^{\tau_{L,l,e}}\left\|Y_{nj}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tau_{L,l,e}-\tau_{L,l,s}\right)^{-1}\sum_{j=\tau_{L,l,s}+1}^{\tau_{L,l,e}}\left\|Y_{nj}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]\right\}\right|\\
=&\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\big((L_{\max}\log L_{\max})^{1/2}\big).\end{split}

Proof.

Similarly to the proof of Lemma
5
, we may assume without loss of generality that
Y_{nj}
is one-dimensional.

We consider the first bound to begin with. For any
L=1,\ldots,L_{\max}
, let
\mathcal{T}_{L}
denote the
\sigma
-algebra generated by
\tau_{L,1,s}
,
\tau_{L,1,e}
,
\tau_{L,2,s}
,
\tau_{L,2,e},\ldots,\tau_{L,L_{\max},s}
,
\tau_{L,L_{\max},e}
. Then, it follows from a union bound and the law of total probability that for any
\xi>0
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{L=1,\ldots,L_{\max}}\sum_{l=1}^{L_{\max}}\left(\tau_{L,l,e}-\tau_{L,l,s}\right)^{-1}\left(\sum_{j=\tau_{L,l,s}+1}^{\tau_{L,l,e}}Y_{nj}\right)^{2}>\xi\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{L=1}^{L_{\max}}\mathbb{P}\left(\sum_{l=1}^{L_{\max}}\left(\tau_{L,l,e}-\tau_{L,l,s}\right)^{-1}\left(\sum_{j=\tau_{L,l,s}+1}^{\tau_{L,l,e}}Y_{nj}\right)^{2}>\xi\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{L=1}^{L_{\max}}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{P}\left(\left.\sum_{l=1}^{L_{\max}}\left(\tau_{L,l,e}-\tau_{L,l,s}\right)^{-1}\left(\sum_{j=\tau_{L,l,s}+1}^{\tau_{L,l,e}}Y_{nj}\right)^{2}>\xi\right|\mathcal{T}_{L}\right)\right].

In the following we focus on the conditional probability inside the expectation. Conditional on
\mathcal{T}_{L}
,
\left(\tau_{L,l,e}-\tau_{L,l,s}\right)^{-1}\left(\sum_{j=\tau_{L,l,s}+1}^{\tau_{L,l,e}}Y_{nj}\right)^{2},\ l=1,\ldots,L_{\max}
are independent. Bernstein’s inequality, see Theorem 2.10 in
boucheron2013concentration
, yields that
\left(\tau_{L,l,e}-\tau_{L,l,s}\right)^{-1/2}\sum_{j=\tau_{L,l,s}+1}^{\tau_{L,l,e}}Y_{nj}
is sub-exponentially distributed with Orlicz norm bounded uniformly over
l=1,\ldots,L_{\max},\ L=1,\ldots,L_{\max}
, i.e. there exist a constant
\psi^{(1)}<\infty
such that

\inf\left\{C\in(0,\infty)\,:\,\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(C^{-1}\left(\tau_{L,l,e}-\tau_{L,l,s}\right)^{-1}\left(\sum_{j=\tau_{L,l,s}+1}^{\tau_{L,l,e}}Y_{nj}\right)^{2}\right)\right]\leq 2\right\}\leq\psi^{(1)}

for all
l=1,\ldots,L_{\max},\ L=1,\ldots,L_{\max}
. Consequently, it follows from Corollary 3 in
zhang2021sharper
that
\left(\tau_{L,l,e}-\tau_{L,l,s}\right)^{-1}\left(\sum_{j=\tau_{L,l,s}+1}^{\tau_{L,l,e}}Y_{nj}\right)^{2}
are sub-Weibull distributed with parameter
\theta=1/2
and uniformly bounded sub-Weibull norms, i.e. there exist a constant
\psi^{(1/2)}<\infty
such that

\inf\left\{C\in(0,\infty)\,:\,\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(C^{-1/2}\left(\left(\tau_{L,l,e}-\tau_{L,l,s}\right)^{-1}\left(\sum_{j=\tau_{L,l,s}+1}^{\tau_{L,l,e}}Y_{nj}\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}\right)\right]\leq 2\right\}\leq\psi^{(1/2)}

for all
l=1,\ldots,L_{\max},\ L=L1,\ldots,L_{\max}
. Hence, it follows from Corollary 6.4 in
zhang2020concentration
(see also Theorem 1 in
zhang2021sharper
) that there exist constants
C_{1},C_{2}>0
(not depending on
L
) such that for any
t>0
,

\mathbb{P}\left(\left.\sum_{l=1}^{L_{\max}}\left(\tau_{L,l,e}-\tau_{L,l,s}\right)^{-1}\left(\sum_{j=\tau_{L,l,s}+1}^{\tau_{L,l,e}}Y_{nj}\right)^{2}\geq C_{1}\sqrt{L_{\max}t}+C_{2}t^{2}\right|\mathcal{T}_{L}\right)\leq 2e^{-t}

(73)

for all
L=1,\ldots,L_{\max}
. Thus, for any
a>1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\max_{L=1,\ldots,L_{\max}}\sum_{l=1}^{L_{\max}}\left(\tau_{L,l,e}-\tau_{L,l,s}\right)^{-1}\left(\sum_{j=\tau_{L,l,s}+1}^{\tau_{L,l,e}}Y_{nj}\right)^{2}>a\left[C_{1}\left(L_{\max}\log L_{\max}\right)^{1/2}+C_{2}\left(\log L_{\max}\right)^{2}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{L=1}^{L_{\max}}2\exp\left(-\sqrt{a}\log L_{\max}\right)=2L_{\max}^{-\sqrt{a}+1}.

The r.h.s. can be made arbitrarily small by choosing
a
large enough, thus showing
(
72
)
.

We will now show the second bound. From following the same steps as before we see that it suffices to show that

\sum_{l=1}^{L_{\max}}\left\{\left(\tau_{L,l,e}-\tau_{L,l,s}\right)^{-1}\sum_{j=\tau_{L,l,s}+1}^{\tau_{L,l,e}}\left\|Y_{nj}\right\|_{2}^{2}-\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\tau_{L,l,e}-\tau_{L,l,s}\right)^{-1}\sum_{j=\tau_{L,l,s}+1}^{\tau_{L,l,e}}\left\|Y_{nj}\right\|_{2}^{2}\right]\right\}

conditional on
\mathcal{T}_{L}
is sub-Weibull distributed with parameter
\theta=1/2
and uniformly bounded sub-Weibull norms.

It follows from the assumptions that the
\left\|Y_{nj}\right\|_{2}
’s are independent and sub-exponentially distributed with uniformly bounded Orlicz norms. Consequently, it follows from Corollary 3 in
zhang2021sharper
that the
\left\|Y_{nj}\right\|_{2}^{2}
’s are sub-Weibull distributed with parameter
\theta=1/2
and uniformly bounded sub-Weibull norms. The same applies for the centred random variables and the mean of those centred random variables. Hence, the bound follows from the same arguments as used to derive the bound for the first term.
∎

Lemma 9
.

For any
L\in\mathbb{N}
and any sequence of time points
1\leq a=:t_{0}<t_{1}<\cdots<t_{L}<t_{L+1}:=b\leq n
and any vectors
X_{1},\ldots,X_{n}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
we have that

\displaystyle S_{X}\left(\left\{a,b\right\}\right)-S_{X}\left(\left\{t_{0},t_{1},\ldots,t_{L},t_{L+1}\right\}\right)\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{l=0}^{L}\frac{(b-a)-(t_{l+1}-t_{l})}{b-a}(t_{l+1}-t_{l})\left(\overline{X}_{t_{l}:t_{l+1}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{l=0}^{L}(t_{l+1}-t_{l})\left(\overline{X}_{t_{l}:t_{l+1}}\right)^{2}.

Proof.

From (
31
), we have that

\displaystyle S_{X}\left(\left\{a,b\right\}\right)-S_{X}\left(\left\{t_{0},t_{1},\ldots,t_{L},t_{L+1}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{l=0}^{L}(t_{l+1}-t_{l})\left(\overline{X}_{t_{l}:t_{l+1}}\right)^{2}-(b-a)\left(\overline{X}_{a:b}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{l=0}^{L}(t_{l+1}-t_{l})\left(\overline{X}_{t_{l}:t_{l+1}}\right)^{2}-\sum_{l_{1}=0}^{L}\sum_{l_{2}=0}^{L}\frac{(t_{l_{1}+1}-t_{l_{1}})(t_{l_{2}+1}-t_{l_{2}})}{b-a}\overline{X}_{t_{l_{1}}:t_{l_{1}+1}}\overline{X}_{t_{l_{2}}:t_{l_{2}+1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{l=0}^{L}\left(1-\frac{t_{l+1}-t_{l}}{b-a}\right)(t_{l+1}-t_{l})\left(\overline{X}_{t_{l}:t_{l+1}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle-\sum_{l=0}^{L}(b-a)^{-1}\sum_{l^{{}^{\prime}}=0,\ldots,L;\ l^{{}^{\prime}}\neq l}(t_{l^{{}^{\prime}}+1}-t_{l^{{}^{\prime}}})(t_{l+1}-t_{l})\overline{X}_{t_{l}:t_{l+1}}\overline{X}_{t_{l^{{}^{\prime}}}:t_{l^{{}^{\prime}}+1}}.

Using the inequality
2xy\leq x^{2}+y^{2}
yields

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{l=0}^{L}(b-a)^{-1}\sum_{l^{{}^{\prime}}=0,\ldots,L;\ l^{{}^{\prime}}\neq l}(t_{l^{{}^{\prime}}+1}-t_{l^{{}^{\prime}}})(t_{l+1}-t_{l})\overline{X}_{t_{l}:t_{l+1}}\overline{X}_{t_{l^{{}^{\prime}}}:t_{l^{{}^{\prime}}+1}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{l=0}^{L}(b-a)^{-1}\sum_{l^{{}^{\prime}}=0,\ldots,L;\ l^{{}^{\prime}}\neq l}(t_{l^{{}^{\prime}}+1}-t_{l^{{}^{\prime}}})(t_{l+1}-t_{l})\left\{\left(\overline{X}_{t_{l}:t_{l+1}}\right)^{2}+\left(\overline{X}_{t_{l^{{}^{\prime}}}:t_{l^{{}^{\prime}}+1}}\right)^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{l=0}^{L}(b-a)^{-1}(t_{l+1}-t_{l})\left(\overline{X}_{t_{l}:t_{l+1}}\right)^{2}\sum_{l^{{}^{\prime}}=0,\ldots,L;\ l^{{}^{\prime}}\neq l}(t_{l^{{}^{\prime}}+1}-t_{l^{{}^{\prime}}}).

The proof is completed by noting that
\sum_{l^{{}^{\prime}}=0,\ldots,L;\ l^{{}^{\prime}}\neq l}(t_{l^{{}^{\prime}}+1}-t_{l^{{}^{\prime}}})=(b-a)-(t_{l+1}-t_{l})<b-a
.
∎

Lemma 10
.

For any
L\in\mathbb{N}
and any sequence of time points
1\leq a=:t_{0}<t_{1}<\cdots<t_{L}<t_{L+1}:=b\leq n
and any vectors
X_{1},\ldots,X_{n}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
and
Y_{1},\ldots,Y_{n}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
we have that

\displaystyle 2\left|S_{X,Y}\left(\left\{t_{0},t_{1},\ldots,t_{L},t_{L+1}\right\}\right)-S_{X,Y}\left(\left\{a,b\right\}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\{S_{X}\left(\left\{a,b\right\}\right)-S_{X}\left(\left\{t_{0},t_{1},\ldots,t_{L},t_{L+1}\right\}\right)\right\}+\left\{S_{Y}\left(\left\{a,b\right\}\right)-S_{Y}\left(\left\{t_{0},t_{1},\ldots,t_{L},t_{L+1}\right\}\right)\right\}.

Proof.

Let
P_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times n}
and
P_{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times n}
be the orthogonal projection matrices that project vectors onto the constant segments given by the change-point sets
\left\{a,b\right\}
and
\left\{t_{0},t_{1},\ldots,t_{L},t_{L+1}\right\}
respectively.
Then,
X^{\top}(I-P_{0})Y=S_{X,Y}\left(\left\{a,b\right\}\right)
and
X^{\top}(I-P_{1})Y=S_{X,Y}\left(\left\{t_{0},t_{1},\ldots,t_{L},t_{L+1}\right\}\right)
. Hence,

\displaystyle 2\left|S_{X,Y}\left(\left\{a,b\right\}-S_{X,Y}\left(\left\{t_{0},t_{1},\ldots,t_{L},t_{L+1}\right\}\right)\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left|X^{\top}(P_{1}-P_{0})Y\right|\leq X^{\top}(P_{1}-P_{0})X+Y^{\top}(P_{1}-P_{0})Y

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\{S_{X}\left(\left\{a,b\right\}\right)-S_{X}\left(\left\{t_{0},t_{1},\ldots,t_{L},t_{L+1}\right\}\right)\right\}+\left\{S_{Y}\left(\left\{a,b\right\}\right)-S_{Y}\left(\left\{t_{0},t_{1},\ldots,t_{L},t_{L+1}\right\}\right)\right\}.

∎

Lemma 11
.

Suppose that Assumptions
1
–
5
hold in the case where
K\geq 1
eventually, or only Assumptions
1
,
2
and
4
in the case
K=0\ \forall\ n
. Then,

(i)

\max_{L=0,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda})
.

(ii)

\max_{L=0,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda})
.

Proof.

To show
(i)
, first let
L\in\{0,\ldots,K_{\max}\}
be fixed. Let
\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}=:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,0}<\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,1}<\cdots<\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,\hat{K}_{l}}<\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,\hat{K}_{l}+1}:=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}
be the true change-points between
\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}
and
\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}
, so

\bigcup_{l=0}^{L}\bigcup_{k=0}^{\hat{K}_{l}}{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}}=\bigcup_{l=0}^{L}{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}}\cup\bigcup_{k=0}^{K}{\tau_{k}^{O}}
.
It follows from Lemma
9
that

\displaystyle 0\leq S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\leq 2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l=0,\ldots,L,\\
\hat{K}_{l}>0\end{subarray}}\sum_{k=0}^{\hat{K}_{l}}\left(\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}-\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}\right)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}}\right)^{2}.

Then, the fact that
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l=0,\ldots,L,\\
\hat{K}_{l}>0\end{subarray}}(\hat{K}_{l}+1)\leq 2K
and Lemma
8
gives us that

\max_{L=0,\ldots,K_{\max}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l=0,\ldots,L,\\
\hat{K}_{l}>0\end{subarray}}\sum_{k=0}^{\hat{K}_{l}}\left(\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}-\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}\right)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}}\right)^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\overline{\sigma}^{2}(K_{\max}\log K_{\max})^{1/2}\right).

Finally, Assumption
1
(iii)
yields
(K_{\max}\log K_{\max})^{1/2}=o(\log\log\overline{\lambda})
.

We now show
(ii)
. Let
L\in\{0,\ldots,K_{\max}\}
be fixed. Let
\tau_{k}^{O}=:\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,0}\leq\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,1}<\cdots<\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,\tilde{L}_{k}}\leq\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,\tilde{L}_{k}+1}:=\tau_{k+1}^{O}
be the estimated change-points in
\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}
between
\tau_{k}^{O}
and
\tau_{k+1}^{O}
, so
\bigcup_{k=0}^{K}\bigcup_{l=0}^{\tilde{L}_{k}}{\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,l}}=\bigcup_{k=0}^{K}{\tau_{k}^{O}}\cup\bigcup_{l=0}^{L}{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}}
and
\sum_{k=0}^{K}L_{k}=L
.
It follows from Lemma
9
that

\displaystyle 0\leq S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\leq 2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=0,\ldots,K,\\
\tilde{L}_{k}>0\end{subarray}}\sum_{l=0}^{\tilde{L}_{k}}\left(\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,l+1}-\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,l}\right)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,l}:\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,l+1}}\right)^{2}.

Then, the fact that
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=0,\ldots,K,\ \tilde{L}_{k}>0\end{subarray}}(\tilde{L}_{k}+1)\leq 2L
and Lemma
8
gives us that

\displaystyle\max_{L=0,\ldots,K_{\max}}\bigg\{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=0,\ldots,K,\\
\tilde{L}_{k}>0\end{subarray}}\sum_{l=0}^{\tilde{L}_{k}}\left(\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,l+1}-\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,l}\right)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,l}:\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,l+1}}\right)^{2}\bigg\}=\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\overline{\sigma}^{2}(K_{\max}\log K_{\max})^{1/2}\right),

and so the result follows similarly to (i).
∎

The following lemma is a uniform version of Lemma 4 in
zou2020consistent
. Note that
(i)
below extends
zou2020consistent
to allow for sequences of index sets
\mathcal{I}_{L}
of missing change-points rather than a fixed single change-point.

Lemma 12
.

Let
E_{i}:=\mu^{O}_{i}+\varepsilon^{E}_{i},\ i=1,\ldots,n/2
. Suppose that Assumptions
1
–
5
hold in the case where
K\geq 1
eventually, or only Assumptions
1
,
2
and
4
in the case
K=0\ \forall\ n
. Then we have the following.

(i)

Suppose that, in addition, for a constant
A>0
, there exist sequences of non-empty sets
\mathcal{I}_{L}\subseteq\{1,\ldots,K\}
such that

\mathbb{P}\Bigg(\forall\ L<K,\ \forall\ k\in\mathcal{I}_{L},\ \sum_{i=\tau_{k}^{O}-\lfloor\frac{\underline{\lambda}}{4}\rfloor+1}^{\tau_{k}^{O}+\lfloor\frac{\underline{\lambda}}{4}\rfloor}{\big(\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{L,i}\big)^{2}}\geq A\underline{\lambda}\Delta_{k}^{2}\Bigg)\to 1,

(74)

with
\overline{\mu}^{O}_{L,i}:=\sum_{l=0}^{L}{\mathbb{1}_{\{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1\leq i\leq\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}\}}\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}}
. Then

\begin{split}\min_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left(\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}}{\Delta_{k}^{2}}\right)^{-1}\left\{S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}\geq\underline{\lambda}\left(A+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)\right).\end{split}

(ii)

\max_{L=0,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{E}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda})
.

(iii)

S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{E}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)=o_{\mathbb{P}}(\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda})
.

Proof.

We will show
(i)
after
(ii)
and
(iii)
, since
(i)
is more complex and uses other results.

Turning to
(ii)
, note that

\displaystyle S_{E}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)=S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right).

Then,
(ii)
follows from Lemma
11
(i)
.

We now show
(iii)
.
It follows from (
31
) that

\begin{split}&S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{E}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\\
=&\sum_{k=0}^{K}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k+1}}\left(\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k+1}}\right)^{2}+2\sum_{k=0}^{K}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k+1}}\left(\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k+1}}\right)\varepsilon^{E}_{i}\\
&-\sum_{k=0}^{K}\left(\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k+1}-\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k}\right)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k+1}}\right)^{2}+\sum_{k=0}^{K}\left(\tau^{O}_{k+1}-\tau^{O}_{k}\right)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\tau^{O}_{k}:\tau^{O}_{k+1}}\right)^{2}\\
=:&A_{1}+2A_{2}+A_{3}+A_{4}.\end{split}

By Markov’s inequality,
A_{3}=\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\big(K\overline{\sigma}^{2}\big)
and
A_{4}=\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\big(K\overline{\sigma}^{2}\big)
.

In the following we will bound
A_{1}
and
A_{2}
. To this end, let
\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k}:=\min\big\{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k},\tau^{O}_{k}\big\}
and
\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}:=\max\big\{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k},\tau^{O}_{k}\big\}
,
k=0\ldots,K+1
. Furthermore, we define
\Omega_{n}
to be the event in Assumption
3
(i)
. Note that
\mathbb{P}(\Omega_{n})\to 1
. In the following we work on
\Omega_{n}
.

From Assumptions
3
(iii)
and
5
, we have that
\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}-\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k}\leq\delta_{0,k}\leq\underline{\lambda}/4
. Consequently,
\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k+1}-\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k}\geq\underline{\lambda}/2
,
k=0,\ldots,K
.

Now for
i\in[\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k},\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k+1}]
we know that
\mu^{O}_{i}=\beta_{k}
, and

\begin{split}\left|\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k+1}}-\beta_{k}\right|\leq&\frac{(\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}-\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k})\Delta_{k}+(\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k+1}-\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k+1})\Delta_{k+1}}{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k+1}-\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k}}\\
\leq&\frac{\delta_{0,k}\Delta_{k}+\delta_{0,k+1}\Delta_{k+1}}{\max\left\{\underline{\lambda}/2,\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k+1}-\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}\right\}},\end{split}

for every
k=0,\ldots,K
, where we have used the notation
\Delta_{0}=\Delta_{K+1}=0
. Thus it follows from the fact that
(x+y)^{2}\leq 2x^{2}+2y^{2}
and
\delta_{0,k}\leq\underline{\lambda}/2
,
k=1,\ldots,K
, that

\begin{split}A_{1}\leq&\sum_{k=0}^{K}\left(\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k+1}-\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}\right)\left(\beta_{k}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k+1}}\right)^{2}\\
&+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}-\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k}\right)\max\left\{\left(\beta_{k}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k-1}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k}}\right)^{2},\left(\beta_{k-1}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k+1}}\right)^{2}\right\}\\
=&\mathcal{O}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}\delta_{0,k}\Delta_{k}^{2}\right).\end{split}

Turning to
A_{2}
, for the following equality we use that, by definition of
\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k}
and
\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}
,
\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k+1}}
is constant between
\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}+1
and
\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k+1}
as well as between
\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k}
and
\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}
.

Next, the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality and
2|xy|\leq x^{2}+y^{2}
yield

\begin{split}\left|A_{2}\right|=&\left|\sum_{k=0}^{K}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k+1}}\left(\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k+1}}\right)\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}:\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k+1}}\right.\\
&+\left.\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k}}^{\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}}\left(\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k+1}}\right)\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k}:\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}}\right|\\
\leq&\sum_{k=0}^{K}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k+1}}\left(\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{K,k+1}}\right)^{2}\\
&+\sum_{k=0}^{K}\left(\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k+1}-\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}\right)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}:\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k+1}}\right)^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}-\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k}\right)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k}:\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}}\right)^{2}.\end{split}

Finally, since
\varepsilon^{O}_{1},\ldots,\varepsilon^{O}_{n/2}
and
\varepsilon^{E}_{1},\ldots,\varepsilon^{E}_{n/2}
are independent, we obtain from Markov’s inequality that

\begin{split}&\sum_{k=0}^{K}\left(\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k+1}-\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}\right)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}:\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k+1}}\right)^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(K\overline{\sigma}^{2}\right),\\
&\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}-\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k}\right)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{\min}_{k}:\hat{\tau}^{\max}_{k}}\right)^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(K\overline{\sigma}^{2}\right).\end{split}

Recall that
\mathbb{P}(\Omega_{n})\to 1
. Thus, in total we obtain

\begin{split}&S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{E}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)=A_{1}+A_{2}+A_{3}+A_{4}=\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}\delta_{0,k}\Delta_{k}^{2}\right)+\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(K\overline{\sigma}^{2}\right).\end{split}

Moreover, Assumption
1
(ii)
yields
K=o(\log\log\overline{\lambda})
. Hence, it follows from Assumption
3
(iv)
that

S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{E}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)=o_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}\right).

We now show
(i)
.
We have that

S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)=\left\{S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{E}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}-\left\{S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{E}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}.

From
(iii)
we have that
S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{E}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)=o_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}\right)
.

Furthermore, it follows from (
31
) that

\begin{split}&S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{E}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\\
=&\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}{\left(\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)^{2}}+2\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}{\left(\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)\varepsilon^{E}_{i}}+S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right).\end{split}

In addition, Lemma
11
yields

\begin{split}&\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left|S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)\right|\\
\leq&\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}+\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}\\
&+\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}+\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}\\
=&o_{\mathbb{P}}(\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}).\end{split}

(75)

Let
\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}
,
l=0,\ldots,L,\ k=0,\ldots,\hat{K}_{l}
, be defined as in the proof of Lemma
11
(i)
. Then,

\displaystyle\min_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left\{S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\min_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{k=0}^{\hat{K}_{l}}\left(\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}-\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}\right)\bigg[\left(\mu^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}+1}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 150.0pt+2\left(\mu^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}+1}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+o_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log(\overline{\lambda})\right).

Next, it follows from the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality that

\begin{split}&\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{k=0}^{\hat{K}_{l}}{\left(\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}-\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}\right)\left(\mu^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}+1}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}}}\\
\geq&-\left(\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{k=0}^{\hat{K}_{l}}\left(\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}-\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}\right)\left(\mu^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}+1}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}\\
&\left(\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{k=0}^{\hat{K}_{l}}\left(\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}-\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}\right)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}}\right)^{2}\right)^{1/2}.\end{split}

Then, Lemma
8
yields

\begin{split}\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{k=0}^{\hat{K}_{l}}\left(\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}-\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}\right)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}}\right)^{2}=\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\overline{\sigma}^{2}(K\log K)^{1/2}\right),\end{split}

since
\sum_{l=0}^{L}(\hat{K}_{l}+1)\leq K+L\leq 2K
. In addition, Assumption
1
(iii)
yields that

(K\log K)^{1/2}\leq(K_{\max}\log K_{\max})^{1/2}=o(\log\log\overline{\lambda}).

Moreover,
|\mathcal{I}_{L}|\geq 1
and Assumption
5
yield

\displaystyle\min_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\Big(\underline{\lambda}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}}{\Delta_{k}^{2}}\Big)^{-1}\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}\leq\left(\underline{\lambda}\Delta_{(1)}^{2}\right)^{-1}\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}\to 0.

In addition, it follows from the definition of
\overline{\mu}^{O}_{L,i}
and (
74
) that

\begin{split}&\min_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left\{\Big(\underline{\lambda}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}}{\Delta_{k}^{2}}\Big)^{-1}\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{k=0}^{\hat{K}_{l}}{\left(\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}-\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}\right)\left(\mu^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}+1}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)^{2}}\right\}\\
=&\min_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left\{\Big(\underline{\lambda}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}}{\Delta_{k}^{2}}\Big)^{-1}\sum_{k=0}^{K}\sum_{i=\tau_{k}^{O}+1}^{\tau_{k+1}^{O}}(\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{L,i})^{2}\right\}\\
\geq&\min_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left\{\Big(\underline{\lambda}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}}{\Delta_{k}^{2}}\Big)^{-1}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}}\sum_{i=\tau_{k}^{O}-\lfloor\frac{\underline{\lambda}}{4}\rfloor+1}^{\tau_{k}^{O}+\lfloor\frac{\underline{\lambda}}{4}\rfloor}(\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{L,i})^{2}\right\}\geq A,\end{split}

with probability approaching 1.
Thus,

\min_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left\{\Big(\underline{\lambda}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}}{\Delta_{k}^{2}}\Big)^{-1}\left(S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)\right)\right\}\geq A+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1).

∎

The following lemma is a uniform version of Lemma 5 in
zou2020consistent
.

Lemma 13
.

Suppose that Assumptions
1
–
5
hold in the case where
K\geq 1
eventually, or only Assumptions
1
,
2
and
4
in the case
K=0\ \forall\ n
. Then,

(i)

\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}=\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}(K\overline{\sigma}^{2}(\log\overline{\lambda})^{2})
and

\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}=\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}(K\overline{\sigma}^{2}(\log\overline{\lambda})^{2})
,

(ii)

S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)=o_{\mathbb{P}}(\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda})
,

(iii)

\max_{L=K,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda})
.

Proof.

Without loss of generality we may assume
K>0
, since the statements are trivial for
K=0
.

(i)
Let
L\in\{0,\ldots,K-1\}
be fixed. Recall that
\tau_{k}^{O}=:\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,0}\leq\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,1}<\cdots<\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,\tilde{L}_{k}}\leq\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,\tilde{L}_{k}+1}:=\tau_{k+1}^{O}
denote the estimated change-points in
\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}
between
\tau_{k}^{O}
and
\tau_{k+1}^{O}
, so
\bigcup_{k=0}^{K}\bigcup_{l=0}^{\tilde{L}_{k}}{\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,l}}=\bigcup_{k=0}^{K}{\tau_{k}^{O}}\cup\bigcup_{l=0}^{L}{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}}
and
\sum_{k=0}^{K}L_{k}=L
.

Then, Lemma
9
yields

\displaystyle 0\leq

\displaystyle S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\leq 2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=0,\ldots,K,\\
\tilde{L}_{k}>0\end{subarray}}\sum_{l=0}^{\tilde{L}_{k}}\left(\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,l+1}-\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,l}\right)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,l}:\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,l+1}}\right)^{2}.

It follows from
\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=0,\ldots,K,\\
\tilde{L}_{k}>0\end{subarray}}(\tilde{L}_{k}+1)\leq 2L
and Lemma
5
that

\displaystyle\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=0,\ldots,K,\\
\tilde{L}_{k}>0\end{subarray}}\sum_{l=0}^{\tilde{L}_{k}}\left(\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,l+1}-\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,l}\right)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,l}:\tilde{\tau}^{O}_{L,k,l+1}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=0,\ldots,K,\\
\tilde{L}_{k}>0\end{subarray}}(\tilde{L}_{k}+1)\max_{\tau_{k}^{O}\leq i<j\leq\tau_{k+1}^{O}}(j-i)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{i:j}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}2L\max_{k=1,\ldots,K}\max_{\tau_{k}^{O}\leq i<j\leq\tau_{k+1}^{O}}(j-i)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{i:j}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(K\overline{\sigma}^{2}\left((\log\overline{\lambda})^{2}+(\log K)^{2}\right)\right).

Finally, Assumption
1
(ii)
yields that
\log K\leq\log\overline{\lambda}
and the first statement follows.

For the second statement, let
L\in\{0,\ldots,K-1\}
be fixed. Recall that
\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}=:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,0}<\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,1}<\cdots<\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,\hat{K}_{l}}<\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,\hat{K}_{l}+1}:=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}
denote the true change-points between
\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}
and
\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}
, so

\bigcup_{l=0}^{L}\bigcup_{k=0}^{\hat{K}_{l}}{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}}=\bigcup_{l=0}^{L}{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}}\cup\bigcup_{k=0}^{K}{\tau_{k}^{O}}.

Then, Lemma
9
yields

\displaystyle 0\leq

\displaystyle S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\leq 2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l=0,\ldots,L,\\
\hat{K}_{l}>0\end{subarray}}\sum_{k=0}^{\hat{K}_{l}}\left(\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}-\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}\right)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}}\right)^{2}.

It follows from Lemma
6
that

\begin{split}&\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l=0,\ldots,L,\\
\hat{K}_{l}>0\end{subarray}}\sum_{k=0}^{\hat{K}_{l}}\left(\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}-\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}\right)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}}\right)^{2}\\
\leq&\sum_{k=0}^{K}(\tau_{k+1}^{O}-\tau_{k}^{O})\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}:\tau_{k+1}^{O}}\right)^{2}\\
&+\sum_{k=0}^{K}\max_{i=\tau_{k}^{O}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k+1}^{O}-1}(\tau_{k+1}^{O}-i)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{i:\tau_{k+1}^{O}}\right)^{2}+\sum_{k=0}^{K}\max_{i=\tau_{k}^{O}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k+1}^{O}-1}(i-\tau_{k}^{O})\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}:i}\right)^{2}\\
\leq&\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\sqrt{K}\overline{\sigma}^{2}\right)+(K+1)\max_{k=0,\ldots,K}\max_{i=\tau_{k}^{O}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k+1}^{O}-1}\left\{(\tau_{k+1}^{O}-i)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{i:\tau_{k+1}^{O}}\right)^{2}+(i-\tau_{k}^{O})\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}:i}\right)^{2}\right\}\\
=&\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(K\overline{\sigma}^{2}\left(\log\log\overline{\lambda}+(\log K)^{2}\right)\right).\end{split}

(76)

Finally, Assumption
1
(ii)
yields that
\log K\leq\log\overline{\lambda}
and the statement follows.

(ii)
Let
\mathcal{T}^{\delta}_{K}:=\{\tau_{k}^{O}\pm\lfloor\delta_{0,k}\rfloor,\ k=1,\ldots,K\}
and
\mathcal{T}^{\delta}_{L}:=\{\tau_{k}^{O}\pm\lfloor\delta_{1,k}\rfloor,\ k=1,\ldots,K\}
,
L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}
. Then,

\displaystyle S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{\delta}_{K}\right)\right\}-\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{\delta}_{K}\right)\right\}.

We are working on the event in Assumption
3
(i)
, but Assumption
3
(i)
assumes that the probability of that event converges to one. Using similar arguments as in
(i)
gives us

\displaystyle\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{\delta}_{K}\right)\right\}-\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{\delta}_{K}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(K\overline{\sigma}^{2}\left(\log\log\Big(\max_{k=1,\ldots,K}\delta_{0,k}\vee e\Big)+(\log K)^{2}\right)\right).

Finally, Assumption
3
(ii)
gives
K\log\log\Big(\max_{k=1,\ldots,K}\delta_{0,k}\vee e\Big)=o(\log\log\overline{\lambda})
and Assumption
1
(ii)
yields
K(\log K)^{2}=o(\log\log\overline{\lambda})
.

(iii)
For
k=1,\ldots,K
let
\delta_{k}:=\max_{q=0,1}\lfloor\delta_{q,k}\rfloor
. Moreover, let
\mathcal{T}^{\delta}_{L}:=\{\tau_{k}^{O}\pm\delta_{k},\ k=1,\ldots,K\}
,
L=K,\ldots,K_{\max}
. Then,

\displaystyle S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{\delta}_{L}\right)\right\}-\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{\delta}_{L}\right)\right\}.

We will focus on how to bound
\max_{L=K,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{\delta}_{L}\right)\right\}
, but the same bound can be obtained for
\max_{L=K,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{\delta}_{L}\right)\right\}
by using identical arguments.

Let
L\in\{K,\ldots,K_{\max}\}
be fixed. Let
\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}=:\check{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,0}<\check{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,1}<\cdots<\check{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,\check{K}_{l}}<\check{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,\check{K}_{l}+1}:=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}
denote all elements of the set
\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{\delta}_{L}
that are between
\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}
and
\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}
, so

\bigcup_{l=0}^{L}\bigcup_{k=0}^{\check{K}_{l}}{\check{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}}=\bigcup_{l=0}^{L}{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{\delta}_{L}
. Then, Lemma
9
yields

\displaystyle 0\leq

\displaystyle S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{\delta}_{L}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l=0,\ldots,L,\\
\check{K}_{l}>0\end{subarray}}\sum_{k=0}^{\check{K}_{l}}\frac{\left(\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}\right)-\left(\check{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}-\check{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}\right)}{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}}\left(\check{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}-\check{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}\right)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\check{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}:\check{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l=0,\ldots,L,\\
\check{K}_{l}>0\end{subarray}}\sum_{k=0}^{\check{K}_{l}}\left(\check{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}-\check{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}\right)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\check{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k}:\check{\tau}^{O}_{L,l,k+1}}\right)^{2}.

We are working on the event in Assumption
3
(i)
, but Assumption
3
(i)
assumes that the probability of that event converges to one. Hence, for every
k=1,\ldots,K
there exists an
l=1,\ldots,L
(not necessarily unique) such that
\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k}\leq\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}\leq\tau_{k}^{O}
or
\tau_{k}^{O}\leq\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}\leq\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}
. Thus,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\max_{L=K,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{\delta}_{L}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K}(\tau_{k+1}^{O}-\delta_{k+1}-\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k})\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}:\tau_{k+1}^{O}-\delta_{k+1}}\right)^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\delta_{k}\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k}:\tau_{k}^{O}}\right)^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\delta_{k}\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}:\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{t=\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k}^{O}-1}(\tau_{k}^{O}-t)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k}:t}\right)^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{t=\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k}^{O}-1}(t-\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k})\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{t:\tau_{k}^{O}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{t=\tau_{k}^{O}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}-1}(t-\tau_{k}^{O})\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}:t}\right)^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{t=\tau_{k}^{O}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}-1}(\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}-t)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{t:\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{t=\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k+1}^{O}-\delta_{k+1}-1}\frac{(t-\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k})-(t-\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k})}{t-\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}}(t-\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k})\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}:t}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{t=\tau_{k-1}^{O}+\delta_{k-1}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k}-1}\frac{(\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}-t)-(\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k}-t)}{\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}-t}(\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k}-t)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{t:\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k}}\right)^{2},

where we have used the notation
\delta_{0}:=\delta_{K+1}:=0
.

The following calculation follows from a central limit theorem for triangular arrays. Note that Lyapunov’s condition is met, since higher moments are uniformly bounded because of Assumption
2
. We obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{K}(\tau_{k+1}^{O}-\delta_{k+1}-\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k})\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}:\tau_{k+1}^{O}-\delta_{k+1}}\right)^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\delta_{k}\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k}:\tau_{k}^{O}}\right)^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\delta_{k}\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}:\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\sqrt{K}\overline{\sigma}^{2}\right).

Furthermore, Assumption
1
(ii)
yields
\sqrt{K}=o(\log\log\overline{\lambda})
.

It follows from Lemma
6
that

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{t=\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k}^{O}-1}(\tau_{k}^{O}-t)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k}:t}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K\max_{k=1,\ldots,K}\max_{t=\tau_{k}^{O}-\max_{k=1,\ldots,K}\delta_{k}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k}^{O}-1}(\tau_{k}^{O}-t)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}-\max_{k=1,\ldots,K}\delta_{k}:t}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(K\overline{\sigma}^{2}\left(\log\log\Big(\max_{k=1,\ldots,K}\delta_{k}\vee e\Big)+(\log K)^{2}\right)\right).

Furthermore, Assumption
1
(ii)
yields
K(\log K)^{2}=o(\log\log\overline{\lambda})
and Assumption
3
(ii)
yields

K\log\log\Big(\max_{k=1,\ldots,K}\delta_{k}\vee e\Big)=o(\log\log\overline{\lambda}).

The same bound applies to the other terms and hence

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{t=\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k}^{O}-1}(\tau_{k}^{O}-t)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k}:t}\right)^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{t=\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k}^{O}-1}(t-\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k})\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{t:\tau_{k}^{O}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{t=\tau_{k}^{O}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}-1}(t-\tau_{k}^{O})\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}:t}\right)^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{t=\tau_{k}^{O}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}-1}(\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}-t)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{t:\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}\right).

Finally, let
C>2
be a constant. Then,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{t=\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k+1}^{O}-\delta_{k+1}-1}\frac{(t-\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k})-(t-\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k})}{t-\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}}(t-\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k})\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}:t}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{t=\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k+1}^{O}-\tau_{k}^{O}+C\{\max_{l=1,\ldots,K}\delta_{l}\}}(t-\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k})\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}:t}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{t=\tau_{k}^{O}+C\{\max_{l=1,\ldots,K}\delta_{l}\}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k+1}^{O}-\delta_{k+1}-1}2\delta_{k}\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}:t}\right)^{2}.

It follows from Lemma
6
that

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{t=\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k+1}^{O}-\tau_{k}^{O}+C\{\max_{l=1,\ldots,K}\delta_{l}\}}(t-\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k})\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}:t}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle K\max_{k=1,\ldots,K}\max_{t=\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k+1}^{O}-\tau_{k}^{O}+C\{\max_{l=1,\ldots,K}\delta_{l}\}}(t-\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k})\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}:t}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(K\overline{\sigma}^{2}\left(\log\log\Big(\max_{k=1,\ldots,K}\delta_{k}\vee e\Big)+(\log K)^{2}\right)\right).

Moreover, it follows from Lemma
7
that

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{t=\tau_{k}^{O}+C\{\max_{l=1,\ldots,K}\delta_{l}\big\}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k+1}^{O}-\delta_{k+1}-1}2\delta_{k}\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}:t}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big\{\max_{l=1,\ldots,K}\delta_{l}\big\}\delta_{k}\max_{t=\tau_{k}^{O}+C\{\max_{l=1,\ldots,K}\delta_{l}\}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k+1}^{O}-\delta_{k+1}-1}\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}:t}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(K\overline{\sigma}^{2}(\log K)^{2}\right).

Thus, similarly to our earlier argument, it follows from Assumptions
3
(ii)
and Assumption
1
(ii)
that

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{t=\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k+1}^{O}-\delta_{k+1}-1}\frac{(t-\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k})-(t-\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k})}{t-\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}}(t-\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k})\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}:t}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{t=\tau_{k-1}^{O}+\delta_{k-1}+1,\ldots,\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k}-1}\frac{(\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}-t)-(\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k}-t)}{\tau_{k}^{O}+\delta_{k}-t}(\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k}-t)\left(\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{t:\tau_{k}^{O}-\delta_{k}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}\right).

This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 14
.

Recall
E_{i}:=\mu^{O}_{i}+\varepsilon^{E}_{i},\ i=1,\ldots,n/2
and
\widetilde{\operatorname{CV}}^{O}_{\mathrm{mod}}(L)
as defined in (
66
). Suppose that Assumptions
1
–
5
hold in the case where
K\geq 1
eventually, or only Assumptions
1
,
2
and
4
in the case
K=0\ \forall\ n
. Then we have the following.

(i)

Suppose that, in addition,
K\geq 1
.
If for some
A>0
,
\mathcal{I}_{L}\subseteq\{1,\ldots,K\}
for
L=0,\ldots,K-1
is a sequence of non-empty sets satisfying

\mathbb{P}\Bigg(\forall\ L<K,\ \forall\ k\in\mathcal{I}_{L},\ \sum_{i=\tau_{k}^{O}-\lfloor\frac{\underline{\lambda}}{4}\rfloor+1}^{\tau_{k}^{O}+\lfloor\frac{\underline{\lambda}}{4}\rfloor}{\big(\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{L,i}\big)^{2}}\geq A\underline{\lambda}\Delta_{k}^{2}\Bigg)\to 1,

then

\displaystyle\min_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\Bigg(\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}}{\Delta_{k}^{2}}\Bigg)^{-1}\left\{\widetilde{\operatorname{CV}}^{O}_{\mathrm{mod}}(L)-\widetilde{\operatorname{CV}}^{O}_{\mathrm{mod}}(K)\right\}\geq\underline{\lambda}\left(A+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)\right).

(ii)

\displaystyle\min_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{\widetilde{\operatorname{CV}}^{O}_{\mathrm{mod}}(L)-\widetilde{\operatorname{CV}}^{O}_{\mathrm{mod}}(K)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\min_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}\left(1+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)\right).

Proof.

As seen in (
69
), we have for any
L\neq K
,

\displaystyle\widetilde{\operatorname{CV}}^{O}_{\mathrm{mod}}(L)-\widetilde{\operatorname{CV}}^{O}_{\mathrm{mod}}(K)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\{S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}-\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}-\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle+2\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle=:A_{1}+A_{2}+A_{3}+2A_{4}.

In the following we will derive uniform bounds for each of the
A_{j}
separately.

Showing
(i)
:

Let
K\geq 1
. We have that

\displaystyle S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)=\left\{S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{E}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}-\left\{S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{E}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}.

Bounding
A_{1}
:
It follows from Lemma
12
,
(i)
,
(iii)
, the fact that
|\mathcal{I}_{L}|\geq 1
and Assumption
5
, that

\displaystyle\min_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\Bigg(\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}}{\Delta_{k}^{2}}\Bigg)^{-1}\left\{S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}\geq\underline{\lambda}\left(A+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)\right).

(77)

Bounding
A_{2}
:
It follows from Lemma
13
that

\begin{split}&\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left|S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)\right|\\
\leq&\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}+\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}\\
&+\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}+\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}\\
=&\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}(K\overline{\sigma}^{2}(\log\overline{\lambda})^{2}).\end{split}

(78)

Bounding
A_{3}
:
A bound for the third term is given in (
75
). We have that

\begin{split}&\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}).\end{split}

(79)

Bounding
A_{4}
:
It follows from Lemma
10
that

\begin{split}&2\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left|S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)\right|\\
\leq&2\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left|S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right|\\
&+2\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left|S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right|\\
&+2\left|S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right|+2\left|S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)\right|\\
\leq&\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}+\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}\\
&+\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}+\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}\\
&+\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}+\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}\\
&+\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}+\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}\\
=&\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(K\overline{\sigma}^{2}(\log\overline{\lambda})^{2}\right).\end{split}

(80)

where the last bound is a consequence of Lemmas
9
,
11
and

13
as seen before.

Thus, by combining (
77
)–(
80
) we have that

\displaystyle\min_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\Bigg(\underline{\lambda}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}}{\Delta_{k}^{2}}\Bigg)^{-1}\left\{\widetilde{\operatorname{CV}}^{O}_{\mathrm{mod}}(L)-\widetilde{\operatorname{CV}}^{O}_{\mathrm{mod}}(K)\right\}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle A+o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)+\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\Bigg(\min_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\Bigg(\underline{\lambda}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}}{\Delta_{k}^{2}}\Bigg)^{-1}K\overline{\sigma}^{2}(\log\overline{\lambda})^{2}\Bigg).

Hence,
(i)
follows from the fact that
|\mathcal{I}_{L}|\geq 1
and Assumption
5
.

Showing
(ii)
:

Bounding
A_{1}
:
It follows from Lemma
12
(ii)
and
(iii)
that

\begin{split}&\min_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}\\
\geq&\min_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}\\
&-\max_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{E}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}-\left|S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{E}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right|\\
\geq&-\max_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{E}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}-\left|S_{E}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{E}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right|\\
=&-\left|o_{\mathbb{P}}(\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda})\right|.\end{split}

(81)

Bounding
A_{2}
:
It follows from Lemma
13
that

\begin{split}&\min_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)\right\}\\
\geq&-\max_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}+\min_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}\\
&-\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}-\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}\\
=&\min_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}+o_{\mathbb{P}}(\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}).\end{split}

(82)

Bounding
A_{3}
:
It follows from Lemma
11
that

\begin{split}&-\min_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}=\max_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}\\
\leq&\max_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}+\max_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}\\
&+\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}+\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}\\
=&o_{\mathbb{P}}(\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}).\end{split}

(83)

Bounding
A_{4}
:
We have that

\displaystyle 2\left|S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\left|S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right|+2\left|S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right|

\displaystyle+2\left|S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right|+2\left|S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)\right|.

Moreover, it follows from the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality that

\displaystyle\left|S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right)^{1/2}\left(S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right)^{1/2}

Thus, it follows from Lemmas
10
,
9
,
11
and
13
that

\begin{split}&\min_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left|S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O},\varepsilon^{E}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{K}\right)\right|\\
=&o_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\min_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}\right)+o_{\mathbb{P}}(\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}).\end{split}

(84)

Thus, by combining (
81
)–(
84
) we have that

\displaystyle\min_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{\widetilde{\operatorname{CV}}^{O}_{\mathrm{mod}}(L)-\widetilde{\operatorname{CV}}^{O}_{\mathrm{mod}}(K)\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\min_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle+o_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\min_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left\{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right\}\right)+o_{\mathbb{P}}(\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}).

Hence,
(ii)
follows from Assumption
4
.
∎

Lemma 15
.

Suppose that Assumptions
1
–
5
hold in the case where
K\geq 1
eventually.
Let

\displaystyle B_{1,L,l}^{(n)}:=

\displaystyle\left|\overline{\mu}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right|,

\displaystyle B_{2,L,l}^{(n)}:=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\left|\mu^{E}_{i}-\mu^{O}_{i}\right|},

\displaystyle B_{3,L,l}^{(n)}:=

\displaystyle\max_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1,\ldots,\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\left|\mu^{E}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}\right|},

\displaystyle B_{4,L,l}^{(n)}:=

\displaystyle\max_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1,\ldots,\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}{\left|\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right|},

and

\displaystyle B_{L}^{(n)}:=\sum_{l=0}^{L}\max\left(B_{1,L,l}^{(n)},B_{2,L,l}^{(n)},B_{3,L,l}^{(n)},B_{4,L,l}^{(n)}\right)^{2}.

Then we have the following.

(i)

\max_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left(S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right)^{-1}\max\Big(B_{K}^{(n)},B_{L}^{(n)}\Big)=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1).

(ii)

When
K\geq 1
, there exists a constant
A>0
such that defining

\overline{\mu}^{O}_{L,i}:=\sum_{l=0}^{L}{\mathbb{1}_{\{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1\leq i\leq\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}\}}\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}}

and

\mathcal{I}_{L}:=\Bigg\{k:\sum_{i=\tau_{k}^{O}-\lfloor\frac{\underline{\lambda}}{4}\rfloor+1}^{\tau_{k}^{O}+\lfloor\frac{\underline{\lambda}}{4}\rfloor}{\big(\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{L,i}\big)^{2}}\geq A\underline{\lambda}\Delta_{k}^{2}\Bigg\},

we have that
\mathcal{I}_{L}\neq\emptyset
for all
L\leq K-1
and all
n
, and

\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\Bigg(\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}}{\Delta_{k}^{2}}\Bigg)^{-1}\max\Big(B_{K}^{(n)},B_{L}^{(n)}\Big)=o_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\underline{\lambda}\right).

Proof.

First note that if
K=0
for all
n
, then
B^{(n)}_{L}=0
for all
L
and so the claims hold trivially. Now suppose
K\geq 1
eventually and let the event
\Omega_{n}
be the intersection of the events in
(i)
and
(v)
of Assumption
3
. We note that
\Omega_{n}
must have probability converging to
1
. In the following we work on
\Omega_{n}
.

Proof of (i).

Let us fix
L\geq K
. For each
l=0,\ldots,L
, let
k=k(l)\in\{1,\ldots,K+1\}
be such that
\tau_{k-1}^{O}\leq\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}<\tau_{k}^{O}
.
Further, when
k\neq K+1
, due to the fact that we are on
\Omega_{n}
, we have that there exists some
\tilde{l}
such that

\tau_{k}^{O}\leq\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,\tilde{l}}\leq\tau_{k+2}^{O}
as
\min(\tau_{k+2}^{O}-\tau_{k+1}^{O},\tau_{k+1}^{O}-\tau_{k}^{O})\geq\underline{\lambda}/2-1
.

Therefore, exactly one of the following scenarios must occur

(1)

\tau_{k-1}^{O}\leq\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}<\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}\leq\tau_{k}^{O}
,

(2)

\tau_{k-1}^{O}\leq\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}<\tau_{k}^{O}<\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}\leq\tau_{k+1}^{O}
,

(3)

\tau_{k-1}^{O}\leq\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}<\tau_{k}^{O}<\tau_{k+1}^{O}<\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}\leq\tau_{k+2}^{O}
.

We observe that
\mu^{E}_{i}-\mu^{O}_{i}=\mu_{2i}-\mu_{2i-1}=\beta_{k}-\beta_{k-1}
if
\tau_{k}=2i-1
and zero if such a
k
does not exist. Hence,
|\mu^{E}_{i}-\mu^{O}_{i}|\leq\Delta_{k}
if
\tau_{k}^{O}=i
and zero if such a
k
does not exist. Therefore,

if (1) occurs, then

\max\left(B_{1,L,l}^{(n)},B_{2,L,l}^{(n)},B_{3,L,l}^{(n)},B_{4,L,l}^{(n)}\right)=0;

if (2) occurs, then

\max\left(B_{1,L,l}^{(n)},B_{2,L,l}^{(n)},B_{3,L,l}^{(n)},B_{4,L,l}^{(n)}\right)\leq\Delta_{k(l)};

if (3) occurs, then

\max\left(B_{1,L,l}^{(n)},B_{2,L,l}^{(n)},B_{3,L,l}^{(n)},B_{4,L,l}^{(n)}\right)\leq\Delta_{k(l)}+\Delta_{k(l)+1}.

Also, because of Assumption
3
(iv)
, if
\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}/(K\Delta_{k(l)}^{2})\leq C_{n}
, then there exists an
\tilde{l}
such that
\tau_{k(l)}^{O}=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,\tilde{l}}
. Consequently, scenario (1) must occur. From the same reasoning, we have that scenario (3) cannot occur if
\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}/(K\Delta_{k(l)+1}^{2})\leq C_{n}
. Moreover, by the structure of the scenarios, if
k(l_{1})=k(l_{2})
for any
l_{1}\neq l_{2}
, then scenario (1) must occur for
l_{1}
or
l_{2}
(or for both). Thus,

\max_{L=K,\ldots,K_{\max}}B_{L}^{(n)}\mathbb{1}_{\Omega_{n}}\leq 4K\ \left(C_{n}^{-1}\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}/K\right)=o\left(\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}\right).

(85)

Thus we obtain from Assumption
4
and
\mathbb{P}(\Omega_{n})\to 1
that

\max_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\frac{\max\Big(B_{L}^{(n)},B_{K}^{(n)}\Big)}{S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)}=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1)

as required.

Proof of (ii).

Let us set
A=\min(1/200,C)
. Fix
L<K
and
k\in\{1,\ldots,K\}
. Note that

\min_{c\in\mathbb{R}}\sum_{i=\tau_{k}^{O}-\lfloor\frac{\underline{\lambda}}{4}\rfloor+1}^{\tau_{k}^{O}+\lfloor\frac{\underline{\lambda}}{4}\rfloor}{(\mu^{O}_{i}-c)^{2}}\geq A\underline{\lambda}\Delta_{k}^{2}.

Consequently, since
L<K
, it follows that
\mathcal{I}_{L}
is non-empty. Furthermore, note that Assumption
3
(v)
is also true when
C
is replaced by
A
.

Let
0=\hat{k}_{0}\leq\cdots\leq\hat{k}_{L+1}=K+1
be such that
\tau_{\hat{k}_{l}}^{O}\leq\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}<\tau_{\hat{k}_{l}+1}^{O}
. Then for each
l=0,\ldots,L
we have that
\tau_{\hat{k}_{l}}^{O}\leq\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}<\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}<\tau_{\hat{k}_{l+1}+1}^{O}
. Recall that
|\mu^{E}_{i}-\mu^{O}_{i}|\leq\Delta_{k}
if
\tau_{k}^{O}=i
and zero if such a
k
does not exist. Therefore,

\max\left(B_{1,L,l}^{(n)},B_{2,L,l}^{(n)},B_{3,L,l}^{(n)},B_{4,L,l}^{(n)}\right)\leq\sum_{k=\hat{k}_{l}+1}^{\hat{k}_{l+1}}\Delta_{k}.

Recall that we are working on
\Omega_{n}
. Due to Assumption
3
(v)
, for each
k\not\in\mathcal{I}_{L}
we have that either
k\notin\mathcal{K}_{n}
so
\Delta_{k}^{2}<\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}/(KC_{n})
or there exists an
l=1,\ldots,L
such that
\tau_{k}^{O}=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}
. Hence,

\max\left(B_{1,L,l}^{(n)},B_{2,L,l}^{(n)},B_{3,L,l}^{(n)},B_{4,L,l}^{(n)}\right)\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=\hat{k}_{l}+1,\ldots,\hat{k}_{l+1},\\
k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}\end{subarray}}{\Delta_{k}}+\big(\hat{k}_{l+1}-\hat{k}_{l}\big)\sqrt{\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}/(KC_{n})}.

Consequently, by using
(\sum_{i=1}^{n}a_{i})^{2}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}na_{i}^{2}
for any
a_{1},\ldots,a_{n}\in\mathbb{R}
,we obtain

\begin{split}B_{L}^{(n)}\leq&\sum_{l=0}^{L}{\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=\hat{k}_{l}+1,\ldots,\hat{k}_{l+1},\\
k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}\end{subarray}}{\Delta_{k}}+\big(\hat{k}_{l+1}-\hat{k}_{l}\big)\sqrt{\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}/(KC_{n})}\Bigg)^{2}}\\
\leq&2\sum_{l=0}^{L}{\Bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=\hat{k}_{l}+1,\ldots,\hat{k}_{l+1},\\
k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}\end{subarray}}{\Delta_{k}}\Bigg)^{2}}+\sum_{l=0}^{L}{2\big(\hat{k}_{l+1}-\hat{k}_{l}\big)^{2}\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}/(KC_{n})}\\
\leq&2K\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}}{\Delta_{k}^{2}}+2K\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}.\end{split}

Recall that
\mathcal{I}_{L}
is non-empty. Hence,
\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}}{\Delta_{k}^{2}}\geq\Delta_{(1)}^{2}
.
Thus, Assumption
5
yields

K\overline{\sigma}^{2}\log\log\overline{\lambda}\leq K\overline{\sigma}^{2}(\log\overline{\lambda})^{2}=o_{\mathbb{P}}\bigg(\min_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\underline{\lambda}\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}}{\Delta_{k}^{2}}\bigg)

and hence because of Assumption
1
(ii)
,

\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\Bigg(\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}}{\Delta_{k}^{2}}\Bigg)^{-1}B_{L}^{(n)}=o_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\underline{\lambda}\right).

From (
85
) and Assumption
5
we also obtain that

\max_{L=0,\ldots,K-1}\Bigg(\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}}{\Delta_{k}^{2}}\Bigg)^{-1}\max\Big(B_{L}^{(n)},B_{L}^{(n)}\Big)=o_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\underline{\lambda}\right).\qed

Lemma 16
.

Recall
\operatorname{CV}^{O}_{\mathrm{mod}}(L)
and
\widetilde{\operatorname{CV}}^{O}_{\mathrm{mod}}(L)
from (
65
) and (
66
), respectively. Suppose that Assumptions
1
–
5
hold in the case where
K\geq 1
eventually, or only Assumptions
1
,
2
and
4
in the case
K=0\ \forall\ n
. Let

A_{L}^{(n)}:=\left|\operatorname{CV}^{O}_{\mathrm{mod}}(L)-\widetilde{\operatorname{CV}}^{O}_{\mathrm{mod}}(L)\right|.

Then,

\max_{L=K+1,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left(S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)-S_{\varepsilon^{O}}\left(\hat{\mathcal{T}}^{O}_{L}\cup\mathcal{T}^{O}_{K}\right)\right)^{-1}\max\big(A_{L}^{(n)},\,A_{K}^{(n)}\big)=o_{\mathbb{P}}(1).

Furthermore, there exists a constant
A>0
such that writing

\mathcal{I}_{L}:=\Bigg\{k:\sum_{i=\tau_{k}^{O}-\lfloor\frac{\underline{\lambda}}{4}\rfloor+1}^{\tau_{k}^{O}+\lfloor\frac{\underline{\lambda}}{4}\rfloor}{\big(\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{L,i}\big)^{2}}\geq A\underline{\lambda}\Delta_{k}^{2}\Bigg\},

where

\overline{\mu}^{O}_{L,i}:=\sum_{l=0}^{L}{\mathbb{1}_{\{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1\leq i\leq\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}\}}\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}}
, we have that
\mathcal{I}_{L}\neq\emptyset
for all
L\leq K-1
and all
n
, and

\max_{L=0\ldots,K-1}\Bigg(\sum_{k\in\mathcal{I}_{L}}{\Delta_{k}^{2}}\Bigg)^{-1}\max\big(A_{L}^{(n)},\,A_{K}^{(n)}\big)=o_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\underline{\lambda}\right).

(86)

Proof.

We will split
A_{L}^{(n)}
into a sum of five terms and show the statement for each of them separately, thus giving the result. Note that the existence of a constant
A>0
such that
\mathcal{I}_{L}
is non-empty is guaranteed by Lemma
15
.

Splitting
A_{L}^{(n)}

We have

\begin{split}A_{L}^{(n)}\leq&\left|\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\frac{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}}{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}-1}\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}-\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)^{2}}-\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}{\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}-\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)^{2}}\right|\\
&+\left|\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\frac{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}}{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}-1}\left[2\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}-\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)\left(\mu^{E}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)+\left(\mu^{E}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)^{2}\right]}\right.\\
&-\left.\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}{\left[2\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}-\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)\left(\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)+\left(\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)^{2}\right]}\right|.\end{split}

Also,

\begin{split}&\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\frac{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}}{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}-1}\left[2\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}-\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)\left(\mu^{E}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)+\left(\mu^{E}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)^{2}\right]}\\
=&\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\frac{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}}{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}-1}\left[2\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}-\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)\left(\mu^{E}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}\right)+\left(\mu^{E}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}\right)^{2}\right]}\\
&+2\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\frac{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}}{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}-1}\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}-\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)\left(\overline{\mu}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)}\\
&+\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\frac{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}}{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}-1}\left(\overline{\mu}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)^{2}}\\
=&\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\frac{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}}{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}-1}\left[2\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}-\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}\right)\left(\mu^{E}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}\right)+\left(\mu^{E}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}\right)^{2}\right]}\\
&+2\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\frac{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}}{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}-1}\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}-\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)\left(\overline{\mu}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)}\\
&+\sum_{l=0}^{L}\hat{n}^{O}_{l}\left(\overline{\mu}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)^{2}.\end{split}

To see the last equation, note that

\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\left(\mu^{E}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}\right)}=0=\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}\left(\mu^{E}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}\right)}.

Hence,

\begin{split}A_{L}^{(n)}\leq&\left|\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\frac{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}}{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}-1}\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}-\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)^{2}}-\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}{\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}-\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)^{2}}\right|\\
&+\left|\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\frac{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}}{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}-1}\left(\mu^{E}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}\right)^{2}}-\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}{\left(\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)^{2}}\right|\\
&+2\left|\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\frac{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}}{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}-1}\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}-\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}\right)\left(\mu^{E}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}\right)}\right.\\
&-\left.\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}{\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}-\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)\left(\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)}\right|\\
&+2\left|\sum_{l=0}^{L}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\frac{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}}{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}-1}\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}-\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)\left(\overline{\mu}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)}\right|\\
&+\left|\sum_{l=0}^{L}\hat{n}^{O}_{l}\left(\overline{\mu}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)^{2}\right|\\
=:&A_{1,L}^{(n)}+A_{2,L}^{(n)}+2A_{3,L}^{(n)}+2A_{4,L}^{(n)}+A_{5,L}^{(n)},\end{split}

where we have used

\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\overline{\varepsilon}^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\left(\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)}=0=\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}{\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\left(\mu^{O}_{i}-\overline{\mu}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)}.

We will bound each of the five terms in the following.

Bounding
A_{1,L}^{(n)}

\begin{split}A_{1,L}^{(n)}\leq&\left|\sum_{l=0}^{L}\left\{{\frac{1}{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}-1}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}\right)^{2}}-\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)^{2}}\right\}\right|\\
&+2\left|\sum_{l=0}^{L}{\overline{\varepsilon}^{O}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}:\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\left(\frac{1}{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}-1}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\varepsilon^{E}_{i}}-\varepsilon^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)}\right|=:A_{11,L}^{(n)}+2A_{12,L}^{(n)}.\end{split}

(87)

We will now work on both terms separately and then bound the sum of them at the end.

Bounding
A_{11,L}^{(n)}

Let
\mathcal{E}^{O}
be the sigma algebra generated by
\varepsilon^{O}_{1},\ldots,\varepsilon^{O}_{n/2}
. Then,

\begin{split}A_{11,L}^{(n)}=&\left|\sum_{l=0}^{L}{\left\{\frac{1}{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}-1}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}\right)^{2}}-\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)^{2}\right\}}\right|\\
\leq&\left|\sum_{l=0}^{L}{\left\{\frac{1}{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}-1}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}\right)^{2}}-\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\frac{1}{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}-1}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}\right)^{2}}\right|\mathcal{E}^{O}\right]\right\}}\right|\\
&+\left|\sum_{l=0}^{L}{\left\{\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)^{2}-\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)^{2}\right|\mathcal{E}^{O}\right]\right\}}\right|\\
&+\sum_{l=0}^{L}{\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}}\right)^{2}\right|\mathcal{E}^{O}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\frac{1}{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}-1}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}\right)^{2}}\right|\mathcal{E}^{O}\right]\right|}.\end{split}

(88)

In the following we bound the r.h.s. in (
88
). It follows from Lemma
8
that

\begin{split}&\max_{L=0,\ldots,K_{\max}}\left|\sum_{l=0}^{L}{\left\{\frac{1}{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}-1}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}\right)^{2}}-\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\frac{1}{\hat{n}^{O}_{l}-1}\sum_{i=\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l}+1}^{\hat{\tau}^{O}_{L,l+1}-1}{\left(\varepsilon^{E}_{i}\right)^{2}}\right|\mathcal{E}^{O}\right]\right\}}\right|\\
=&\mathcal{O}_{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left(K_{\max}\log K_{\max}\right)^{1/2}\,\overline{\sigma}^{2}\right)\end{split}

and
